This strongly suggests an issue with how the text input is being committed before the save action is triggered. The keyboard reappearing and the "Front" field being focused after a failed save attempt reinforces this theory.

A common way to do this is to explicitly move the focus away from the text input field to another UI element before attempting to save. Let's try clicking on the "Tags" field. This action should shift the focus, hopefully committing the text in the "Front" and "Back" fields.
